  m_g=np.zeros(n); v_g=np.zeros(n); t=0
  for step in range(steps):
    a = softmax(y)
    c = np.convolve(a,a)
    M = c.max()
    sc = 2*n*M  # since sum1
    if sc<best_sc: update
    beta = beta_schedule; lse = logsumexp(c*beta) maybe c values ~1/n 0.002; beta up to n? If beta large. We want weights on top lags. M~0.001 for n=512 target; beta*M maybe?     d = 2*np.correlate(w, a, mode='valid') # length n
    # Smoothed gradient wrt a: d (maybe /beta; absorb lr)

Helper:
```
def _score_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=0: return inf
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    return float(2*a.size* c.max()/(S*S))
```
Note if length n 0 no. For internal a normalized sum1, score=2n*c.max(). But if Adam softmax sum exactly1, okay.

`_normalize(a)`: a=np.clip(a,0,None); S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12 return uniform; return a/S.

Let's think about Adam stability. After each step, compute new a=softmax(y). If score exact inf? no. If y drifts large causing under/overflow in softmax, subtract logsum. Implement stable:
```
def softmax(y):
  z=y - np.max(y)
  E=np.exp(z)
  return E/E.sum()
```
If some y very negative, zero okay; no issue. But if all -inf? no; y finite. If some weights zero, gradient may be zero for them; can add tiny base? We can initialize with positive all. During updates, y might become very low but a positive underflow to0; then can't recover. To avoid, clip y difference to [-30,30] each step: `y = np.clip(y, np.max(y)-30, np.max(y))`. Softmax then min 9e-14. Or maintain y relative with max0, clip min -30. This keeps positivity.

Adam may be enough. Let's write a function `_adam_refine(a0, max_steps, deadline, rng, verbose=False)` returns best a.
Pseudo:
```
def _adam_refine(a0, max_steps, deadline, rng, base_lr=0.2):
    n=len(a0); a0=normalize(a0)
    y=np.log(a0.copy()); y-=y.max(); y=np.clip(y,-50,0)
    best=a0.copy(); best_sc=_score_normalized(a0)
    m_g=np.zeros(n); v_g=np.zeros(n); t=0
    no_imp=0; T=0.01
    for it in range(max_steps):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      a=softmax(y)
      c=np.convolve(a,a)
      M=float(c.max()); sc=2.0*n*M
      if sc < best_sc: best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc; no_imp=0; T=max(0.001, T*0.999)
      else: no_imp+=1; T=max(0.001, T*0.999)
      # beta increases with no_imp
      beta = n * (1.0 + 0.1*no_imp) maybe up to 10n
      bc=beta*c; bc-=bc.max(); e=np.exp(bc); w=e/e.sum()
      d=2.0*np.correlate(w,a,'valid')
      g=a*(d - np.dot(d,a))
      t+=1
      m_g=0.9*m_g+0.1*g; v_g=0.999*v_g+0.001*g*g
      lr=base_lr/ (1+0.01*it)
      step=lr*m_g/(np.sqrt(v_g)+1e-8)
      y_new=y-step
      # If no improvement for long, random nudge
      if no_imp > 30:
          y_new = y_new + rng.normal(0, 0.05/(1+no_imp/50), size=n)
      # clip relative
      y_new -= y_new.max(); y_new=np.clip(y_new, -50, 0)
      # adaptive accept
      a_new=softmax(y_new)
      c_new=np.convolve(a_new,a_new)
      sc_new=2.0*n*c_new.max()
      if sc_new < sc or (no_imp>20 and rng.random() < np.exp(-(sc_new-best_sc)/max(T,1e-6))):
          y=y_new
      # optional block random if stuck
    return best,best_sc
```

Random block multiplicative from previous:
```
def _block_perturb(a, rng, block_count=...):
  n=len(a); a=a.copy()
  # scale a small number of blocks by exp(normal(0,sigma))
  nblocks = rng.integers(2, max(4,n//32))
  for _ in range(nblocks):
    width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//500), max(2,n//20)))
    start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
    a[start:start+width]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1))
  a=normalize(a)
  return a
```

def _pair_transfer(a, field, rng, frac=0.1):
  n=len(a); order maybe choose i from top dec, j from bottom inc.
  # weighted by softmax(field) and softmax(-field)
  p_i=softmax(field*50) maybe field nonnegative; p_j=softmax(-field*50)
  i=rng.choice(n,p=p_i); j=rng.choice(n,p=p_j)
  if i==j return a
  t=min(a[i]*0.05, a[j]*0.5? maybe allow increase target)
  a[i]-=t; a[j]+=t; if a[i]<0 or a[j]>1000 invalid
  normalize.
```

Variant builder details:
```
def _candidate_from_params(n, params, rng=None):
  idx = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
  x = idx/(n-1) if n>1 else np.array([0.])
  alpha = params['alpha']
  shift = params['shift']
  v = (idx + 1.0 + shift)**(-alpha)
  # multiplicative smooth
  u = params['u']; s = params['s']
  if u != 0 and s != 0:
      base = 1.0 + u*x
      lo = base.min()
      if lo <= 1e-6: return None
      v = v * np.power(base, s)
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): return None
  r = params.get('r',0)
  if r: v *= np.exp(r*x)
  b = params.get('b',0)
  if b:
     level=params.get('c',0.7); width=params.get('w',0.25)
     if width<=0: width=0.25
     v *= (1.0 + b*np.exp(-0.5*((x-level)/width)**2))
  par = params.get('par',0)
  if par:
      alt = 1.0 + par*((-1.0)**idx)
      if alt.min() <= 1e-6: return None
      v *= alt
  v = np.clip(v,0,None)
  if v.sum()<1e-12: return None
  return v/v.sum()
```
Parameter ranges:
alpha 0.35-0.7, shift 0-50, u -0.5 to 2, s -1 to 1, r -2 to 2, b 0 to 3, c 0.1 to0.9, w 0.05 to0.4, par -0.3 to0.3. Too many random. We can sample 200 random with many zeros. Instead create base random: choose one modulation type:
- 0: pure
- 1: interval factor
- 2: exp
- 3: bump
- 4: par
Sample fit.

Refinement function with exact score and best preservation.

Let's write `_refine_adam(a0, budget_s)` using deadline absolute. We need compute field. Since a normalized, score_exact = 2*n*c.max(). If sum not exactly1 due to normalize, use internal formula with S to be safe: `score = 2*n*M/S^2`. For softmax sum1, but after random perturb normalize. Keep S.

Implement `_exact_score(a)`:
```
S=a.sum()
if S < 1e-12: return inf
c=np.convolve(a,a)
M=float(c.max())
return float(2*a.size*M/(S*S))
```
In Adam loop, recompute sum each time (1). Use `_score_normalized` to avoid S (maybe c.max()*2n). But after softmax sum exactly due to normalization, fine. For random perturb use exact.

Adam exact acceptance: To save computations, compute current score at start of loop using c from previous? Let's structure:
```
y=...
a=softmax(y)
c=np.convolve(a,a); sc=score_norm(c.max())
best=...
for:
   # use c,sc current
   beta...
   w...
   d...
   g...
   y_new...
   a_new...
   c_new...
   sc_new=...
   accept?
   y=y_new; a=a_new; c=c_new; sc=sc_new
   if sc<best: update

For n = n_main, define `family_add(r, n)`:
```
idx=np.arange(n,float)
v = r/np.sqrt(idx+1.0) + (n-1.0-idx)/np.sqrt(float(n))
# The second term nonnegative; if n=1 use zeros.
a=v/v.sum()
return a
```

def _additive_score(n, r):
   idx=np.arange(n,float)
   v = r/np.sqrt(idx+1.0) + (n-1.0-idx)/np.sqrt(n)
   v = v/v.sum()
   c=np.convolve(v,v); M=c.max(); return 2*n*M
```

def _golden_min(f, a, b, tol=1e-4, max_iter=60, deadline=None):
    gr=(sqrt(5)-1)/2
    c=b-gr*(b-a); d=a+gr*(b-a)

def _random_polish(a0, deadline, rng, max_iters=2000):
  best=a0.copy(); best_sc=score(a0)
  a=a0.copy()
  for it in range(max_iters):
    if time... break
    cand=a.copy()
    # perturb k random blocks
    n=len(cand)
    nb=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//32)))? but cost
    for _ in range(nb):
      width=int(rng.integers(max(1, n//500), max(2, n//30)))
      start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
      cand[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.05 + 0.05/rng?)))
    cand=normalize(cand)
    sc=score(cand)
    if sc < best_sc: best=cand; best_sc=sc
    # update current: if sc < score(a) or accept anneal
    if sc < score_a or rng.random()<0.1: a=cand; ...
  return best
```

Let's implement `_field(w,a)`:
```
def _field(w,a):
  n=len(a); L=2*n-1
  if n < 1024:
     return np.correlate(w,a,'valid')
  size = 1 << (L+len(a)-1 -1).bit_length()
  W=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
  A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
  full=np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(A), size)
  return full[n-1:n-1+n]
```

Let's implement a fast exact score for normalized/unnormalized using `v` and sum:

def _score_unnorm(v):
  S=v.sum()
  if S <=0: return inf
  c=np.convolve(v,v)
  return float(2*v.size*c.max()/(S*S))
```

def _polish(a0, stop_at, rng):
  best=a0; best_sc=score(best); a=best.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
  it=0
  while time<stop_at:
    cand=a.copy()
    n=len(cand)
    # random block multiplicative
    for _ in range(3):
      width=max(1, n//100)
      start=rng.integers(0,n-width+1)
      cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.08))
    cand=normalize(cand)
    sc=score(cand)
    if sc < best_sc: best=cand; best_sc=sc
    if sc < cur_sc or (it>20 and rng.random()<0.05): a=cand; cur_sc=sc
    it+=1
  return best
```

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

_INF = float('inf')

def _score_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return _INF
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return _INF
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-300:
        return _INF
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * a.size * M / (S*S))
```
For normalized arrays, ok. Evaluator clips to [0,1000]; our a <=1, okay.

`_normalize(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,None); S=a.sum(); if S<=0: return np.ones(n)/n; return a/S
```

`_candidate_additive(n,r,p,shift)`:
```
idx=np.arange(n,float); x=idx/(n-1) if n>1 else np.array([0.])
left=1.0/np.sqrt(idx+1.0+shift)
right = ((1.0 - x) ** p) / math.sqrt(float(n))
v=r*left + right
if v sum <=0 return None
return v/v.sum()
```
For p=0 and x maybe slightly >1 due to float? x=1 at i=n-1 exactly if float division by n-1; 1-x=0; 0**0 yields 1 in numpy? In Python 0**0=1; numpy 0.0**0.0 =1? I think yes. If p=0, right constant 1/√n. If p<0 not used. If p=2, zero at endpoint. Good.

`_search_additive(n, stop_at)`:
```
def _search_additive(n, stop_at):
  if n < 2:
    a=np.ones(1); return a, _score_array(a)
  idx=np.arange(n,float); x=np.linspace(0,1,n) # exact endpoints
  inv_sqrt_n=1.0/math.sqrt(n)
  pvals=[0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0]
  shifts=[0.0, 1.0, 5.0]
  rgrid=np.geomspace(0.05, 8.0, 20)
  best_a=None; best_sc=_INF
  # helper eval_v
  def eval_v(v):
     S=float(np.sum(v))
     if S <=0: return _INF
     c=np.convolve(v,v)
     return float(2.0*n*np.max(c)/(S*S))
  # cache left per shift
  lefts={}
  for sh in shifts: lefts[sh]=1.0/np.sqrt(idx+1.0+sh)
  # pure harmonic
  v=lefts[0.0]; sc=eval_v(v); update
  for p in pvals:
    if time.monotonic()>stop_at: break
    right = ((1.0 - x) ** p) * inv_sqrt_n
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(right))? p=0 okay.
    sr=float(right.sum())
    for sh in shifts:
      if time>stop_at: break
      left=lefts[sh]; sl=float(left.sum())
      local_best_r=None; local_best_sc=_INF; local_best_v=None
      for r in rgrid:
         if time>stop_at: break
         v = r*left + right
         S = r*sl + sr
         if S <= 0: continue
         c=np.convolve(v,v); sc=float(2.0*n*np.max(c)/(S*S))
         if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc=sc; best_a=v/S; local...
      # fine refine if local_best_r
      if local_best_r is not None and time<stop_at:
         fine_r = local_best_r * (10.0 ** np.linspace(-0.25,0.25,15))
         for r in fine_r: ...
  if best_a is None: a=np.ones(n)/n; best_sc=2.0
  return best_a, best_sc
```
The update inside local for global best should use `v/S` copy; if store `v/S`, v is view? S copy. Use `best_a = v / S` creates new array. Good.

For p=0, right constant; fine.

Potential issue: `np.geomspace` with 0.05 to8.0 20 points; for r=0.05, v=0.05 left+right; okay. If rgrid[0] small, sequence mostly right; score maybe high but finite.

`_adam_refine(a0, stop_at, rng)`:
```
def _adam_refine(a0, stop_at, rng):
  a=np.asarray(a0,float); a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
  if n < 2: return a
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_array(a)
  y=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); y-=y.max(); y=np.clip(y,-50,0)
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
  no_imp=0; start_no=no_imp
  phases = [(800, 0.12, 3.0), (1200, 0.04, 6.0), (1500, 0.015, 10.0)]
  for max_steps, base_lr, beta_mult in phases:
    if time>stop_at: break
    for it in range(max_steps):
      if time.monotonic()>stop_at: break
      # current score? We need current a. We'll compute c,sc at start of each iteration.
      c=np.convolve(a,a); sc=float(2*n*np.max(c))  # sum1
      if sc < best_sc:
         best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc; no_imp=0
      else:
         no_imp += 1
      # If no improvement for long, random nudge with falling sigma
      sigma = 0.0
      if no_imp > 40:
          sigma = 0.05 / (1.0 + no_imp/50.0)
      beta = beta_mult * n
      bc = beta * c
      bc_max = bc.max()
      e = np.exp(bc - bc_max)
      s = e.sum()
      if not finite or s==0: w=e/e.sum()? else
      w = e/s
      d = 2.0 * np.correlate(w, a, mode='valid')
      # d length n? If due to floating maybe; if len != n, use direct loop? We know n.
      mean = float(np.dot(d, a))
      g = a * (d - mean)
      t += 1
      m = 0.9*m + 0.1*g
      v = 0.999*v + 0.001*g*g
      lr = base_lr / math.sqrt(t/10.0 + 1.0)? or decay step
      step = lr * m / (np.sqrt(v) + 1e-8)
      y_new = y - step
      if sigma > 0:
         y_new = y_new + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
      y_new -= y_new.max()
      y_new = np.clip(y_new, -50.0, 0.0)
      z=y_new - y_new.max(); E=np.exp(z); a_new=E/E.sum()
      c_new=np.convolve(a_new,a_new); sc_new=float(2*n*np.max(c_new))
      # accept if improves current or early anneal
      if sc_new < sc or (no_imp > 60 and rng.random() < 0.03):
          a=a_new; y=y_new
      # if accepted and sc_new < current_sc? no need
    # after phase, reinit from best
    a = best.copy()
    y = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); y-=y.max(); y=np.clip(y,-50,0)
    m=v=0; t=0; no_imp=0
  return best
```
This recomputes c every iteration and c_new; 2 conv per iter, plus correlate. Could be double cost. We can avoid recomputing current c each iter by maintaining c,sc as before. Let's optimize to one conv/iter + c_new conv = 2 total? In previous, we had current c from previous new; still need c_new. So 1 conv per iteration if maintain. Let's restructure:
```
a=...; c=np.convolve(a,a); sc=score
for:
  # use current c
  ... compute y_new
  a_new=...
  c_new=np.convolve(a_new,a_new); sc_new=...
  if accept: a=a_new; c=c_new; sc=sc_new
  # if rejected, current unchanged
  # update best based on current (or new? if not accepted, current unchanged; if accepted, check)
  if sc < best_sc: best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
```
But if rejected, current remains; next iteration repeats same current c and same gradient unless y_new changed? y unchanged, but no_imp and beta maybe same, will generate same step (Adam momentum evolves? We update m,v only if accepted? If rejected, should we update Adam state? If y unchanged, using same gradient but momentum maybe should not update; we can skip m update on rejection to avoid endless same? But if skip, next identical. Need vary: when reject, we can add small noise to y_new next time, or update a different metric. In code above we update m,v regardless of acceptance even if y unchanged, so step changes due to momentum, but still gradient same. It may keep trying similar direction but with different magnitude; okay. But if sc_new worse and rejected, no change; m update based on gradient. Since gradient same, m changes towards same, step maybe; not beneficial. Better when rejected, perform a small random perturbation to y_new or accept uphill if no_imp. Simpler: always accept if sc_new <= sc * (1+1e-9); if rejected, add noise to y_new in same iteration? Could make sc_new different? We can on rejection, `y = y + rng.normal(0, noise)` (drift) and recompute? That costs. To avoid complexity, set acceptance to always accept y_new if sc_new not much worse within small threshold; hill climbing with random noise. Since best preserved, wandering okay. Use condition:
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _score_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * a.size * M / (S * S))


def _normalise(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size))
    out = a / S
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(out)):
        out = np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size))
    return out


def _search_additive_family(n, stop_at, full=True):
    if n < 2:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        return a, _score_array(a)

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    invsqrt_n = 1.0 / math.sqrt(float(n))

    if full:
        pvals = [0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0]
        shifts = [0.0, 0.5, 2.0, 8.0]
        r_count = 20
        refine = True
    else:
        pvals = [0.5, 1.0, 1.5]
        shifts = [0.0, 2.0]
        r_count = 14
        refine = False

    best_a = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    left_cache = {}
    for sh in shifts:
        left_cache[sh] = 1.0 / np.sqrt(idx + 1.0 + sh)

    def _eval_v(v):
        S = float(np.sum(v))
        if S <= 0.0:
            return float("inf")
        c = np.convolve(v, v)
        return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c) / (S * S))

    # Pure decreasing harmonic root as a safe strong start.
    left0 = left_cache[0.0]
    sc = _eval_v(left0)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        best_a = left0 / float(np.sum(left0))
        best_sc = sc

    rgrid = np.geomspace(0.05, 8.0, r_count)

    for p in pvals:
        if time.monotonic() > stop_at:
            break
        if p == 0.0:
            right = np.full(n, invsqrt_n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            right = ((1.0 - x) ** p) * invsqrt_n
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(right)):
            continue
        sr = float(np.sum(right))

        for sh in shifts:
            if time.monotonic() > stop_at:
                break
            left = left_cache[sh]
            sl = float(np.sum(left))

            local_best_r = None
            local_best_sc = float("inf")

            for r in rgrid:
                if time.monotonic() > stop_at:
                    break
                v = r * left + right
                S = r * sl + sr
                if S <= 0.0:
                    continue
                c = np.convolve(v, v)
                sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c) / (S * S))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = v / S
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_sc:
                    local_best_sc = sc
                    local_best_r = r

            if refine and local_best_r is not None and time.monotonic() < stop_at:
                fine_r = local_best_r * (10.0 ** np.linspace(-0.25, 0.25, 13))
                for r in fine_r:
                    if time.monotonic() > stop_at:
                        break
                    v = r * left + right
                    S = r * sl + sr
                    if S <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    c = np.convolve(v, v)
                    sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c) / (S * S))
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_a = v / S

    if best_a is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)):
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        best_sc = 2.0
    return best_a, best_sc


def _adam_refine(a0, stop_at, rng):
    a = _normalise(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_array(best)

    def _init_y_from(arr):
        y = np.log(np.maximum(arr, 1e-300))
        y -= np.max(y)
        return np.clip(y, -50.0, 0.0)

    # Phases: (max_steps, base_lr, beta_multiplier).
    phases = [
        (700, 0.12, 3.0),
        (1100, 0.05, 6.0),
        (1400, 0.018, 10.0),
    ]

    for max_steps, base_lr, beta_mult in phases:
        if time.monotonic() > stop_at:
            break

        a = best.copy()
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))

        y = _init_y_from(a)
        m_g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v_g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        t = 0
        no_imp = 0

        for it in range(max_steps):
            if time.monotonic() > stop_at:
                break

            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = a.copy()
                no_imp = 0
            else:
                no_imp += 1

            beta = float(beta_mult) * float(n)
            bc = beta * c
            bc_shift = bc - np.max(bc)
            e = np.exp(bc_shift)
            es = float(np.sum(e))
            if not np.isfinite(es) or es <= 0.0:
                break
            w = e / es

            # d_i = sum_k w_k * (T_k a)_i = sum_j w_{i+j} a_j.
            d = 2.0 * np.correlate(w, a, mode="valid")
            if d.size != n:
                d = d[:n] if d.size > n else np.pad(d, (0, n - d.size))

            mean_d = float(np.dot(d, a))
            g = a * (d - mean_d)

            t += 1
            m_g = 0.90 * m_g + 0.10 * g
            v_g = 0.999 * v_g + 0.001 * (g * g)
            lr = base_lr / math.sqrt(1.0 + 0.08 * it)
            step = lr * m_g / (np.sqrt(v_g) + 1e-8)

            y_new = y - step
            if no_imp > 35:
                noise_sigma = 0.035 / (1.0 + 0.02 * no_imp)
                y_new = y_new + rng.normal(0.0, noise_sigma, size=n)

            y_new -= np.max(y_new)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(y_new)):
                break
            y_new = np.clip(y_new, -50.0, 0.0)

            z = y_new - np.max(y_new)
            E = np.exp(z)
            ss = float(np.sum(E))
            if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
                break
            a_new = E / ss
            c_new = np.convolve(a_new, a_new)
            sc_new = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c_new))

            accept = np.isfinite(sc_new) and (sc_new < sc + 1e-12)
            if (not accept) and no_imp > 60 and rng.random() < 0.07:
                accept = True

            if accept:
                a = a_new
                y = y_new
                c = c_new
                sc = sc_new

    return best


def _random_polish(a0, stop_at, rng, max_iters):
    a = _normalise(a0)
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_array(best)
    cur_sc = best_sc

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > stop_at:
            break

        cand = a.copy()
        nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        for _ in range(nblocks):
            if n <= 2:
                width = 1
            else:
                lo = max(1, n // 700)
                hi = max(2, n // 25)
                if lo > hi:
                    hi = lo
                width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
            width = min(width, n)
            start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
            factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.07)))
            cand[start:start + width] *= factor

        cand = _normalise(cand)
        sc = _score_array(cand)

        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = cand.copy()
        if sc < cur_sc or (rng.random() < 0.12):
            a = cand.copy()
            cur_sc = sc

    return best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1300.0
    deadline = start + max(1.0, budget - 20.0)
    target = 1.50274349

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    def _update(a, sc):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        if a is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            return
        if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            return
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = arr.copy()

    # Parent construction, if supplied.
    parent_n = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0:
            parent_n = int(p.size)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            ps = _score_array(p)
            if np.isfinite(ps):
                _update(p, ps)
    except Exception:
        parent_n = None

    # Guaranteed valid fallback.
    fallback = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
    _update(fallback, 2.0)

    # Primary search at a resolution large enough for the continuous limit,
    # but still cheap enough for many dense convolution evaluations.
    n_primary = 4096
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 250.0:
        stop_scan = min(deadline - 550.0, start + 240.0)
        a_primary, sc_primary = _search_additive_family(n_primary, stop_scan, full=True)
        _update(a_primary, sc_primary)

        # Refine the best structured candidate with a smoothed max-convolution Adam step.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 260.0:
            a_refined = _adam_refine(a_primary, deadline - 260.0, rng)
            _update(a_refined, _score_array(a_refined))

    # If still above the target, try a larger additive scan (no expensive Adam
    # at the larger size) and a parent-length scan if useful.
    if best_score > target + 1e-9:
        if parent_n is not None and 512 <= parent_n <= 4096 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
            stop_p = min(deadline - 50.0, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
            a_p, sc_p = _search_additive_family(int(parent_n), stop_p, full=False)
            _update(a_p, sc_p)

        if best_score > target + 1e-9 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
            stop_big = min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 220.0)
            a_big, sc_big = _search_additive_family(8192, stop_big, full=False)
            _update(a_big, sc_big)

    # Final light random polish on the current global best, if it remains affordable.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        nbest = int(best_seq.size)
        if nbest <= 6000:
            max_iters = 500 if nbest <= 3000 else 200
            a_pol = _random_polish(best_seq, deadline - 25.0, rng, max_iters)
            _update(a_pol, _score_array(a_pol))

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    candidate = [float(x) for x in best_seq]

    # Final authoritative validation. Do not return a candidate that the
    # evaluator would reject.
    try:
        final_score = evaluate_sequence(candidate)
    except Exception:
        final_score = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        candidate = [1.0]

    return candidate
```
